Dead Estate released in 1998 for PCs and all available consoles to overwhelming critical and commercial success. The game flew off shelves, becoming an unexpected breakout hit. Anya, along with the rest of the Milkbar Lads team, quickly ascended to billionaire status.

Wanting to use her newfound wealth for good, Anya turned her game development career into a political one. In 2004, Anya ran to be President of the United States  and -- in a historic moment --  won the election, making Anya the first woman to have ever served the position of POTUS.

The majority of the world's political experts, think tanks, and leaders consider Anya's two-term presidency to be the "single most prosperous era that the United States, if not the entire globe, has ever seen."
Dead Estate released in 1998 for PCs and all available consoles to decent critical and commercial success. The game performed better than expected. Anya was hired as an artist and marketer at Milkbar Lads. After President Jack Milkbar left the company in 2003 to follow his dream of becoming a marine biologist, Anya took the title.

To this day, Anya remains President of Milkbar Lads LLC and has helped transform the small studio into a AAA behemoth responsible for a number of critically acclaimed titles such as Dog Plays Baseball, Xtreme Woodcutting, and Dead Estate 2: Earth Dies Screaming.

In addition to her work with Milkbar Lads, Anya has also been in charge of a support group for several individuals who allegedly escaped from the same mansion after she did. Among the survivors is an individual who wished to be identified as "Jeff", who claims that Anya has been incredibly helpful and has shown immense care to everyone in the group.

Axel has been confirmed to have escaped the mansion alive with the help of several other survivors since the incident, but it is unknown whether or not he and Anya have kept in contact.
Dead Estate released in 1998 for PCs to middling commercial success. The game performed well enough for Milkbar Lads to hire Anya as a full-time employee. She currently works a low-level position at the game development studio, but is hoping to work her way up the corporate ladder.

Anya remains somewhat scarred by her experiences inside the mansion, but the support she receives from her friends and coworkers is helping her to move past it. She still wonders what happened to Axel.
After the commercial and critical failure of Dead Estate, Anya never returned to the game development scene. Milkbar Lads went bankrupt soon after. Anya moved back to her parents' house and made the decision to use her portion of the game's meager profits to help put herself through college and she is currently working towards a BA in media production.

She is currently working a part-time job at Duaneburger, a popular regional fast food chain known for their famous cheese curds, to make some cash on the side. Anya remains haunted by her experiences inside the mansion, and she hasn't seen Axel since then.

While Dead Estate maintains a fair difficulty, I felt like I had to rely on saving my progress a little too often. Its a very solid game -- but it could use some improvement in this one regard.

It's been an honor to work on Dead Estate for four years.

From the bottoms of our hearts, thank you all for supporting us.




With the game's full release and six major content updates now behind us, it feels bittersweet finally saying goodbye to this project. At the same time, we're all excited for what's next. We hope you are, too.


These credits mark the conclusion of over four years of work for us at Milkbar Lads. When we first started making Dead Estate in August of 2020 for Newgrounds' annual Halloween competition, none of us anticipated our little webgame evolving into what it is now.


Thank you for playing Dead Estate and seeing it all the way through to the very end.

To the three lights of my life:
the most wonderful children
a father could ever ask for,
Cordelia and Lambert,
and my eternal love, Roselia...
I suppose there's no easy way to put this...
If you're reading this, there has been a
catastrophic failure involving my latest
experiment.
Of course, I've probably been gone quite a while
by the time you will have read this,
but I am writing this just in case
something goes wrong.
As for the experiment,
I've submerged myself in the study of
interdimensional travel over the last
few decades.

I'm sure you're already aware.
My research in the last year has
been especially fruitful.
You've seen some of the creatures
in the house, yes?
These are beings who come from a dimension
that I have recently made contact with.

A realm I've designated ''3X1T.''

Don't ask why.
Using a portal that I set up with Chunks,
I have successfully pulled several creatures
from the 3X1T realm into our own world.

It was impossible to move from our realm
into their realm until very recently.
I was able to send Chunks through the portal
into the 3X1T realm and successfully reel him back
into our own dimension afterwards
with no harm done to him.

This was a MAJOR breakthrough in my research.
Tomorrow, I plan on sending myself
through the portal.

If anything happens,
(which is safe to assume if you're reading this)
I have given Chunks a special serum
that will enable him to protect all of you.
Chunks is also the only one
capable of activating the portal.

It will be far too dangerous for any of you
to come after me in the other dimension,
so the serum should also wipe any memories
he has of working on the portal with me.

This is for YOUR safety.
Finally, if Chunks becomes unruly,
I've also prepared an antidote to revert him
to his original mouse-baby state.

I've left the box containing the antidote
just outside my lab, on the balcony.
The key to this box is, well...
SOMEWHERE in the mansion. I forget.

Just look around, you'll find it.
My memory isn't what it used to be.

I apologize.
In any case, this journal
hopefully won't need to be read.

But in the rare event that any of you find this,
I want you to know one thing:

...I love you three with all of my heart.
P.S.
Roselia, please don't forget to feed Chunks.

Cordelia, please -- for the love of God -- don't
drop out of college.

Lambert, no matter where you are in the world,
never give up the pursuit of knowledge.
P.S. x2
Thank you for providing me with the best years of
my life.

I love you all a million times over.

gml_RoomCC_Room_Steam_Music_0_Create
gml_RoomCC_Room_Steam_Music_1_Create
sh_outline
#define LOWPREC lowp
#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0
#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1
#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4
#define	MATRICES_MAX					5

uniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; 

uniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;
uniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;
uniform float gm_FogStart;
uniform float gm_RcpFogRange;

#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8
#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION


//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8
uniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1

float CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)
{
	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)
	{
		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;
		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);
		return fogfactor;
	}
	else
	{
		return 0.0;
	}
}

vec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);
	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;
	float veclen = length(diffvec);
	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise
	float atten;
	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs
	{
		atten = 0.0;
	}
	else
	{
#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION
	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:
	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))
	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)
	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)
	
		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);
		if (veclen > posrange.w)
		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}	
#else
		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp
#endif
	}
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)
{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space
		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space
		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);
		vec3 ws_normal;
		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;
		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);

		vec3 ws_pos;
		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;

		// Accumulate lighting from different light types
		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		
		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		accumcol *= vertexcolour;
		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;
		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);
		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;
		return accumcol;
	}
	else
	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough vertex shader
//
attribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)
//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.	
attribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)
attribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)

varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);
    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;
    
    v_vColour = in_Colour;
    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;
}


precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

uniform vec2 size;
uniform float thick;
uniform vec3 oColor;
uniform float accuracy;
uniform float tol;
uniform vec4 uvs;

const float rad_circle = 6.28319;

void main()
{
    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );
    bool outline = false;
 
    for(float i=1.0; i<=thick; i++){
        for(float d=0.0; d<rad_circle; d+=rad_circle/accuracy){
			vec2 check_pos = v_vTexcoord + i*vec2(cos(d)*size.x, -sin(d)*size.y);
            vec4 datPixel =  v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, check_pos);
            
			bool out_bound = check_pos.x < uvs.r || check_pos.y < uvs.g || check_pos.x > uvs.b || check_pos.y > uvs.a; 
			
            if (datPixel.a>tol && gl_FragColor.a<=tol && !out_bound){
                outline = true;
                break;
            }
        }
		if (outline) break;
    }
    
    if (outline) gl_FragColor = vec4(oColor.r, oColor.g, oColor.b, 1.0);
}

{

	float veclen = length(diffvec);
	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise
	float atten;

		atten = 0.0;
	}
	else

	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:
	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))
	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)

		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);
		if (veclen > posrange.w)
		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}	
#else
		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp
#endif
	}
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)
{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space
		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space
		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);

		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;

		// Accumulate lighting from different light types
		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		
		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		accumcol *= vertexcolour;
		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;
		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);
		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;
		return accumcol;
	}
	else
	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}
}

#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0
#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1

float4x4 	gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX] : register(c0);

bool 	gm_LightingEnabled;
bool 	gm_VS_FogEnabled;
float 	gm_FogStart;
float 	gm_RcpFogRange;

#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8
float4 gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1

float4 gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range
float4 gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];				// rgb=colour, a=1


// GameMaker reserved and common types/inputs

	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))

		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}

#endif

	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space

		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
attribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)
attribute vec3 in_Normal;                    // (qu,qv,q)
attribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)
//attribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;            // (u,v)       unused in this shader.

varying vec4 v_vColour;
varying vec3 v_vCoord;

void main()
{
    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);
    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;    
    v_vColour = in_Colour;
    v_vCoord = in_Normal;
}


precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
varying vec4 v_vColour;
varying vec3 v_vCoord;

void main()
{
    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vCoord.xy / v_vCoord.z );
}


#version 120
#define LOWPREC 
#define lowp
#define mediump
#define highp
#define precision
#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0
#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1
#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4
#define	MATRICES_MAX					5

uniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; 

uniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;
uniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;
uniform float gm_FogStart;
uniform float gm_RcpFogRange;

#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8
#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION


//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8
uniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1

float CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)
{
	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)
	{
		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;
		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);
		return fogfactor;
	}
	else
	{
		return 0.0;
	}
}

vec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);
	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;
	float veclen = length(diffvec);
	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise
	float atten;
	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs
	{
		atten = 0.0;
	}
	else
	{
#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION
	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:
	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))
	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)
	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)
	
		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);
		if (veclen > posrange.w)
		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}	
#else
		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp
#endif
	}
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)
{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space
		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space
		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);
		vec3 ws_normal;
		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;
		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);

		vec3 ws_pos;
		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;

		// Accumulate lighting from different light types
		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		
		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		accumcol *= vertexcolour;
		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;
		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);
		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;
		return accumcol;
	}
	else
	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSL_ 1
attribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)
attribute vec3 in_Normal;                    // (qu,qv,q)
attribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)
//attribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;            // (u,v)       unused in this shader.

varying vec4 v_vColour;
varying vec3 v_vCoord;

void main()
{
    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);
    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;    
    v_vColour = in_Colour;
    v_vCoord = in_Normal;
}

	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}

uniform float gm_FogStart;
uniform float gm_RcpFogRange;

#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8
#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION


//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8
uniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1

{

	{
		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;
		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);
		return fogfactor;

	else
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * diffusecol;

vec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;
	float veclen = length(diffvec);
	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise
	float atten;
	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs
	{
		atten = 0.0;
	}
	else
	{
#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION
	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:
	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))
	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)
	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)
	
		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);
		if (veclen > posrange.w)
		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}	
#else
		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp
#endif
	}
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)
{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space
		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space
		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);
		vec3 ws_normal;
		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;
		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);

		vec3 ws_pos;
		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;

		// Accumulate lighting from different light types
		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		
		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;
		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);
		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;
		return accumcol;
	}
	else
	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}
}

precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;

uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

{

	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform sampler2D v_chroma;

const float x = 1.164383;
const float y = 1.138393;
const float z = 1.138393;
const vec3 src_bias = vec3(16.0 / 255.0, 128.0 / 255.0, 128.0 / 255.0);
const mat3 src_xform = mat3(1.00000000 * x,  0.00000000 * y,  1.57480000 * z,
							1.00000000 * x, -0.18732427 * y, -0.46812427 * z,
							1.00000000 * x,  1.85560000 * y,  0.00000000 * z);
void main()
{
	float yy = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, vec2(v_vTexcoord.x, v_vTexcoord.y)).r;
	vec2 cbcr = texture2D(v_chroma, vec2(v_vTexcoord.x, v_vTexcoord.y)).rg;
	vec3 yuv = vec3(yy, cbcr);
	yuv -= src_bias;
	yuv *= src_xform;
	gl_FragColor = vec4(yuv, 1.0);
}

	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;
	float veclen = length(diffvec);
	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise
	float atten;
	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs
	{
		atten = 0.0;
	}
	else
	{
#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION
	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:
	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))
	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)
	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)
	
		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);
		if (veclen > posrange.w)
		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}	
#else
		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp
#endif
	}
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)
{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space
		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space
		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);
		vec3 ws_normal;
		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;
		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);

		vec3 ws_pos;
		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;

		// Accumulate lighting from different light types
		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		
		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		accumcol *= vertexcolour;
		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;
		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);
		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;
		return accumcol;
	}
	else
	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough vertex shader
//
attribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)
//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.
attribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)
attribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)

varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);
    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;
    
    v_vColour = in_Colour;
    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;
}

precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

uniform vec2 textureSize;
uniform vec2 lineDistance;
uniform float intensity;

void main()
{
	vec2 shAmount=vec2(1.0/textureSize.x, 1.0/textureSize.y);
	vec2 realPos=vec2(floor(textureSize.x*v_vTexcoord.x), floor(textureSize.y*v_vTexcoord.y));
    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);
	if (gl_FragColor.a==1.) {
		
		if (mod(realPos.x, lineDistance.x)==0.0 || mod(realPos.y, lineDistance.y)==0.0) {
			gl_FragColor.rgb*=(1.-intensity);
		}
		
		if (mod(realPos.y+1., lineDistance.y)==0.0) {
			gl_FragColor.rgb*=intensity*6.;
		}
		
	}
}

#version 120
#define LOWPREC 
#define lowp
#define mediump
#define highp
#define precision
#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0
#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1
#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4
#define	MATRICES_MAX					5

uniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; 

uniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;
uniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;
uniform float gm_FogStart;
uniform float gm_RcpFogRange;

#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8
#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION


//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8
uniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1

float CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)
{
	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)
	{
		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;
		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);
		return fogfactor;
	}
	else
	{
		return 0.0;
	}
}

vec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);
	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;
	float veclen = length(diffvec);
	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise
	float atten;
	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs
	{
		atten = 0.0;
	}
	else
	{
#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION
	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:
	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))
	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)
	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)
	
		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);
		if (veclen > posrange.w)
		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}	
#else
		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp
#endif
	}
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)
{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space
		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space
		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);
		vec3 ws_normal;
		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;
		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);

		vec3 ws_pos;
		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;

		// Accumulate lighting from different light types
		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		
		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		accumcol *= vertexcolour;
		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;
		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);
		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;
		return accumcol;
	}
	else
	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}
}

shFluidWave
precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform float time;
uniform float textureWidth;
uniform float textureHeight;
uniform vec2 waveIntensity;
const float speed = 0.015;
const float xFrequency = 10.0;
const float xSize = 1.0;
const float yFrequency = 20.0;
const float ySize = 0.75;

void main()
{
	float horizontalWave = cos(time * speed + (v_vTexcoord.y * textureHeight)) * waveIntensity.x * (xSize / textureWidth);
	float verticalWave = sin(time * speed + (v_vTexcoord.x * textureWidth)) * waveIntensity.y * (ySize / textureHeight);
    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, vec2(v_vTexcoord.x + horizontalWave, v_vTexcoord.y + verticalWave));
}

shGaussianBlur
precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;

	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform vec3 size; // width, height, radius

const int Quality = 4;
const int Directions = 8;
const float Pi = 6.28318530718; // 2 * pi

void main()
{
    vec2 radius = size.z / size.xy;
    vec4 Color = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);

    float angleStep = Pi / float(Directions);
    float qualityStep = 1.0 / float(Quality);
    float totalWeight = 1.0;

    for (float d = 0.0; d < Pi; d += angleStep) {
        vec2 direction = vec2(cos(d), sin(d)) * radius;
        for (float i = qualityStep; i <= 1.0; i += qualityStep) {
            float weight = 1.0 - i;
            totalWeight += weight;
            Color += texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord + direction * i) * weight;
        }
    }

    Color /= totalWeight;
    gl_FragColor = Color * v_vColour;
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
	vec4 color = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);
	if (!(color.r <= 0.0509 && color.g <= 0.0431 && color.b <= 0.1764)) {
		color.rgb = vec3((color.r + color.g + color.b) / 3.0);
	}
	gl_FragColor = color;
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1

// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
	vec4 color = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);
	if (color.r <= 0.052 && color.g <= 0.044 && color.b <= 0.177) {
		color.rgb = vec3(1.0, 1.0, 1.0);
	} else {
		color.rgb = vec3(0.0509, 0.0431, 0.1764);
	}
    gl_FragColor = color;
}

#define _YY_GLSL_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
	vec4 color = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);
	if (color.r <= 0.052 && color.g <= 0.044 && color.b <= 0.177) {
		color.rgb = vec3(1.0, 1.0, 1.0);
	} else {
		color.rgb = vec3(0.0509, 0.0431, 0.1764);
	}
    gl_FragColor = color;
}

shInvertAlpha
precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform vec3 swapColor;

void main() {
	gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);
	if (gl_FragColor.a > 0.0) {
		gl_FragColor.a = 0.0;
	} else {
		gl_FragColor.a = 1.0;
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform bool forceAlpha;

void main()
{
	vec4 color = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);
	float red = max(color.r, 0.0509);
	float green = max(color.g, 0.0431);
	float blue = max(color.b, 0.1764);
	float alpha = color.a;
	if (forceAlpha) {
		alpha = 1.0;
	}
	vec4 newColor = vec4(red, green, blue, alpha);
	gl_FragColor = newColor;
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform float time;       // Time to animate the noise
uniform float intensity;  // Intensity of the static (range 0.0 to 1.0)
uniform float width;
uniform float height;

// Function to generate pseudo-random numbers
float random(vec2 st) {
    vec2 magic = vec2(12.9898, 78.233); // Magic numbers for hashing
    return fract(sin(dot(st, magic)) * 43758.5453123);
}

void main() {
	gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );
	
    // Normalize fragment coordinates
    vec2 uv = gl_FragCoord.xy / vec2(width, height); // Replace with your resolution
    
    // Add time-based randomness
    float noise = random(uv * time); // Increase the scale for better variation
    
    // Apply intensity to the noise
    noise = step(0.5, noise); // Threshold for black and white noise
    
    // Output the noise as grayscale color
	gl_FragColor.r=noise;
	gl_FragColor.g=noise;
	gl_FragColor.b=noise;
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform vec2 texSize;
uniform float pass1;
uniform float pass2;
uniform float set;
void main()
{
	float texWidth=texSize.x;
	float texHeight=texSize.y;
	float xPosition=floor(v_vTexcoord.x * texWidth);
	float yPosition=floor(v_vTexcoord.y * texHeight);
    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );
	float xMod=(mod(xPosition, 2.0));
	float yMod=(mod(yPosition, 2.0));
	if ((gl_FragColor.a<pass1 && gl_FragColor.a>pass2 && xMod==0.0 && yMod==0.0) || (gl_FragColor.a<=pass2 && mod(xPosition+yMod, 2.0)==0.0)) {
		gl_FragColor.a*=set;
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// Simple passthrough fragment shader
//
precision highp float;

varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform float time;
uniform float intensity;

float random (vec2 st, float ti) {
    return fract(sin(dot(vec2(st.x + ti, st.y + ti), vec2(12.9898 + ti * 0.00001, 78.233 + ti * 0.00001))) * 43758.5453123);
}

void main()
{
    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );
	vec2 pos = gl_FragCoord.xy;
	float rnd = (random(pos, time) - 0.5) * intensity;
	gl_FragColor.r += rnd;
	gl_FragColor.g += rnd;
	gl_FragColor.b += rnd;
	
}

	gl_FragColor.r = swapColor.r;
	gl_FragColor.g = swapColor.g;
	gl_FragColor.b = swapColor.b;
}

shSwapAmount
precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;

	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform vec3 swapColor;
uniform vec3 swapBaseColor;
uniform float swapAmount;
uniform float swapThreshold;

void main() {
	gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);
	float fragColorCombined = (gl_FragColor.r + gl_FragColor.g + gl_FragColor.b);
	float swapBaseColorCombined = (swapBaseColor.r + swapBaseColor.g + swapBaseColor.b);
	if ((abs(fragColorCombined - swapBaseColorCombined) / 3.0) <= swapThreshold) {
		gl_FragColor.r = mix(gl_FragColor.r, swapColor.r, swapAmount);
		gl_FragColor.g = mix(gl_FragColor.g, swapColor.g, swapAmount);
		gl_FragColor.b = mix(gl_FragColor.b, swapColor.b, swapAmount);
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
const int arraySize = 4;
uniform float swapColorsR[arraySize];
uniform float swapColorsG[arraySize];
uniform float swapColorsB[arraySize];
uniform float swapBaseColorsR[arraySize];
uniform float swapBaseColorsG[arraySize];
uniform float swapBaseColorsB[arraySize];
uniform float swapAmounts[arraySize];
uniform float swapThresholds[arraySize];

void main() {
    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord);
    
    float fragColorCombined = (gl_FragColor.r + gl_FragColor.g + gl_FragColor.b);

    for (int i = 0; i < arraySize; i++) {
        if (swapThresholds[i] <= 0.0) break;

        float swapBaseColorCombined = swapBaseColorsR[i] + swapBaseColorsG[i] + swapBaseColorsB[i];
        float colorDifference = abs(fragColorCombined - swapBaseColorCombined) * 0.33333;

        if (colorDifference <= swapThresholds[i]) {
            vec3 swapColor = vec3(swapColorsR[i], swapColorsG[i], swapColorsB[i]);
            gl_FragColor.rgb = mix(gl_FragColor.rgb, swapColor, swapAmounts[i]);
        }
    }
}

shWave
precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform float time;
uniform float textureWidth;
uniform float textureHeight;
uniform vec2 waveIntensity;
const float speed = 0.015;
const float xFrequency = 10.0;
const float xSize = 1.0;
const float yFrequency = 20.0;
const float ySize = 0.75;

void main()
{
    float timeFactor = time * speed;
    float xTexCoord = v_vTexcoord.x * textureWidth;
    float yTexCoord = v_vTexcoord.y * textureHeight;

    float horizontalWave = cos(timeFactor + yTexCoord) * waveIntensity.x * (xSize / textureWidth);
    float verticalWave = sin(timeFactor + xTexCoord) * waveIntensity.y * (ySize / textureHeight);

    gl_FragColor = v_vColour * texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, vec2(v_vTexcoord.x + horizontalWave, v_vTexcoord.y + verticalWave));
}

shYUV
precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;

	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}

	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// CUSTOM fragment shader for handling YUV content
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform sampler2D v_chroma;
const float x = 1.164383;
const float y = 1.138393;
const float z = 1.138393;
const vec3 src_bias = vec3(16.0 / 255.0, 128.0 / 255.0, 128.0 / 255.0);
const mat3 src_xform = mat3(1.00000000 * x,  0.00000000 * y,  1.57480000 * z,
                            1.00000000 * x, -0.18732427 * y, -0.46812427 * z,
			    1.00000000 * x,  1.85560000 * y,  0.00000000 * z);
void main()
{
    float yy = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, vec2(v_vTexcoord.x, v_vTexcoord.y)).r;
    vec2 cbcr = texture2D(v_chroma, vec2(v_vTexcoord.x, v_vTexcoord.y)).rg;
    vec3 yuv = vec3(yy, cbcr);
    yuv -= src_bias;
    yuv *= src_xform;
    gl_FragColor = vec4(yuv, 1.0);
}

#define _YY_GLSL_ 1
//
// CUSTOM fragment shader for handling YUV content
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;
uniform sampler2D v_chroma;
const float x = 1.164383;
const float y = 1.138393;
const float z = 1.138393;
const vec3 src_bias = vec3(16.0 / 255.0, 128.0 / 255.0, 128.0 / 255.0);
const mat3 src_xform = mat3(1.00000000 * x,  0.00000000 * y,  1.57480000 * z,
                            1.00000000 * x, -0.18732427 * y, -0.46812427 * z,
			    1.00000000 * x,  1.85560000 * y,  0.00000000 * z);
void main()
{
    float yy = texture2D(gm_BaseTexture, vec2(v_vTexcoord.x, v_vTexcoord.y)).r;
    vec2 cbcr = texture2D(v_chroma, vec2(v_vTexcoord.x, v_vTexcoord.y)).rg;

    yuv -= src_bias;

__yy_sdf_shader
#define LOWPREC lowp
#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0
#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1
#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4
#define	MATRICES_MAX					5

uniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; 

uniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;
uniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;
uniform float gm_FogStart;
uniform float gm_RcpFogRange;

#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8
#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION


//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8
uniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1

float CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)
{
	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)
	{
		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;
		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);
		return fogfactor;
	}
	else
	{
		return 0.0;
	}
}

vec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);
	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;
	float veclen = length(diffvec);
	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise
	float atten;
	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs
	{
		atten = 0.0;
	}
	else
	{
#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION
	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:
	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))
	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)
	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)
	
		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);
		if (veclen > posrange.w)
		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}	
#else
		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp
#endif
	}
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)
{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space
		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space
		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);
		vec3 ws_normal;
		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;
		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);

		vec3 ws_pos;
		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;

		// Accumulate lighting from different light types
		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		
		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		accumcol *= vertexcolour;
		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;
		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);
		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;
		return accumcol;
	}
	else
	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// SDF vertex shader
//
attribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)
//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.
attribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)
attribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)

varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);
    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;
    
    v_vColour = in_Colour;
    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;
}

precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// SDF fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
	vec4 texcol = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );
	
	float spread = fwidth(texcol.a);	
	spread = max(spread * 0.75, 0.001);	
	texcol.a = smoothstep(0.5 - spread, 0.5 + spread, texcol.a);			
	
	vec4 combinedcol = v_vColour * texcol;
	DoAlphaTest(combinedcol);	
			
    gl_FragColor = combinedcol;
}

#version 120
#define LOWPREC 
#define lowp
#define mediump
#define highp
#define precision
#define	MATRIX_VIEW 					0
#define	MATRIX_PROJECTION 				1
#define	MATRIX_WORLD 					2
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW 				3
#define	MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION 	4
#define	MATRICES_MAX					5

uniform mat4 gm_Matrices[MATRICES_MAX]; 

uniform bool gm_LightingEnabled;
uniform bool gm_VS_FogEnabled;
uniform float gm_FogStart;
uniform float gm_RcpFogRange;

#define MAX_VS_LIGHTS	8
#define MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION


//#define	MAX_VS_LIGHTS					8
uniform vec4   gm_AmbientColour;							// rgb=colour, a=1
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Direction[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];		// normalised direction
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_PosRange[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// X,Y,Z position,  W range
uniform vec4   gm_Lights_Colour[MAX_VS_LIGHTS];			// rgb=colour, a=1

float CalcFogFactor(vec4 pos)
{
	if (gm_VS_FogEnabled)
	{
		vec4 viewpos = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW] * pos;
		float fogfactor = ((viewpos.z - gm_FogStart) * gm_RcpFogRange);
		return fogfactor;
	}
	else
	{
		return 0.0;
	}
}

vec4 DoDirLight(vec3 ws_normal, vec4 dir, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, dir.xyz);
	dotresult = min(dotresult, dir.w);			// the w component is 1 if the directional light is active, or 0 if it isn't
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoPointLight(vec3 ws_pos, vec3 ws_normal, vec4 posrange, vec4 diffusecol)
{
	vec3 diffvec = ws_pos - posrange.xyz;
	float veclen = length(diffvec);
	diffvec /= veclen;	// normalise
	float atten;
	if (posrange.w == 0.0)		// the w component of posrange is 0 if the point light is disabled - if we don't catch it here we might end up generating INFs or NaNs
	{
		atten = 0.0;
	}
	else
	{
#ifdef MIRROR_WIN32_LIGHTING_EQUATION
	// This is based on the Win32 D3D and OpenGL falloff model, where:
	// Attenuation = 1.0f / (factor0 + (d * factor1) + (d*d * factor2))
	// For some reason, factor0 is set to 0.0f while factor1 is set to 1.0f/lightrange (on both D3D and OpenGL)
	// This'll result in no visible falloff as 1.0f / (d / lightrange) will always be larger than 1.0f (if the vertex is within range)
	
		atten = 1.0 / (veclen / posrange.w);
		if (veclen > posrange.w)
		{
			atten = 0.0;
		}	
#else
		atten = clamp( (1.0 - (veclen / posrange.w)), 0.0, 1.0);		// storing 1.0f/range instead would save a rcp
#endif
	}
	float dotresult = dot(ws_normal, diffvec);
	dotresult = max(0.0, dotresult);

	return dotresult * atten * diffusecol;
}

vec4 DoLighting(vec4 vertexcolour, vec4 objectspacepos, vec3 objectspacenormal)
{
	if (gm_LightingEnabled)
	{
		// Normally we'd have the light positions\\directions back-transformed from world to object space
		// But to keep things simple for the moment we'll just transform the normal to world space
		vec4 objectspacenormal4 = vec4(objectspacenormal, 0.0);
		vec3 ws_normal;
		ws_normal = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacenormal4).xyz;
		ws_normal = normalize(ws_normal);

		vec3 ws_pos;
		ws_pos = (gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD] * objectspacepos).xyz;

		// Accumulate lighting from different light types
		vec4 accumcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);		
		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoDirLight(ws_normal, gm_Lights_Direction[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		for(int i = 0; i < MAX_VS_LIGHTS; i++)
		{
			accumcol += DoPointLight(ws_pos, ws_normal, gm_Lights_PosRange[i], gm_Lights_Colour[i]);
		}

		accumcol *= vertexcolour;
		accumcol += gm_AmbientColour;
		accumcol = min(vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0), accumcol);
		accumcol.a = vertexcolour.a;
		return accumcol;
	}
	else
	{
		return vertexcolour;
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSL_ 1
//
// SDF vertex shader
//
attribute vec3 in_Position;                  // (x,y,z)
//attribute vec3 in_Normal;                  // (x,y,z)     unused in this shader.
attribute vec4 in_Colour;                    // (r,g,b,a)
attribute vec2 in_TextureCoord;              // (u,v)

varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
    vec4 object_space_pos = vec4( in_Position.x, in_Position.y, in_Position.z, 1.0);
    gl_Position = gm_Matrices[MATRIX_WORLD_VIEW_PROJECTION] * object_space_pos;
    
    v_vColour = in_Colour;
    v_vTexcoord = in_TextureCoord;
}

#version 120
#define LOWPREC 
#define lowp
#define mediump
#define highp
#define precision
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSL_ 1
//
// SDF fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

void main()
{
	vec4 texcol = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );
	
	float spread = fwidth(texcol.a);	
	spread = max(spread * 0.75, 0.001);	
	texcol.a = smoothstep(0.5 - spread, 0.5 + spread, texcol.a);			
	
	vec4 combinedcol = v_vColour * texcol;
	DoAlphaTest(combinedcol);	
			
    gl_FragColor = combinedcol;
}

__yy_sdf_effect_shader
precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// SDF (with effects) fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

// SDF values are measured from 0 (at the outer edge) to 1 which is the innermost point that can be represented
uniform bool gm_SDF_DrawGlow;				// whether the glow effect is enabled
uniform vec2 gm_SDF_Glow_MinMax;			// the SDF range across which the glow fades
uniform vec4 gm_SDF_Glow_Col;				// the colour of the glow

uniform bool gm_SDF_DrawOutline;			// whether the outline effect is enabled
uniform float gm_SDF_Outline_Thresh;		// the SDF distance which represents the outer edge of the outline
uniform vec4 gm_SDF_Outline_Col;			// the colour of the outline

uniform float gm_SDF_Core_Thresh;			// the SDF distance which represents the outer edge the shape
uniform vec4 gm_SDF_Core_Col;				// the colour of the core part of the shape

void main()
{
	vec4 texcol = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );
		
	float pixelspread = fwidth(texcol.a);	
	pixelspread = max(pixelspread * 0.75, 0.001);	
	
	float blendfactor;
	vec4 currcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, -1.0);
	
	// Handle glow effect
	if (gm_SDF_DrawGlow)
	{		
		if (texcol.a > gm_SDF_Glow_MinMax.x)
		{
			currcol = gm_SDF_Glow_Col;
			currcol.a *= smoothstep(gm_SDF_Glow_MinMax.x, gm_SDF_Glow_MinMax.y, texcol.a);
		}
	}	
	
	// Handle outline effect
	if (gm_SDF_DrawOutline)
	{
		if (texcol.a > (gm_SDF_Outline_Thresh - pixelspread))
		{			
			blendfactor = smoothstep(gm_SDF_Outline_Thresh - pixelspread, gm_SDF_Outline_Thresh + pixelspread, texcol.a);
			if (currcol.a < 0.0)
			{
				currcol = vec4(gm_SDF_Outline_Col.r,gm_SDF_Outline_Col.g,gm_SDF_Outline_Col.b, 0.0);
			}
			currcol = mix(currcol, gm_SDF_Outline_Col, blendfactor);
		}
	}
	
	// Handle inner core
	blendfactor = smoothstep(gm_SDF_Core_Thresh - pixelspread, gm_SDF_Core_Thresh + pixelspread, texcol.a);
	
	if (currcol.a < 0.0)
	{
		currcol = vec4(gm_SDF_Core_Col.r,gm_SDF_Core_Col.g,gm_SDF_Core_Col.b, 0.0);
	}
	texcol = mix(currcol, gm_SDF_Core_Col, blendfactor);	
	
	vec4 combinedcol = v_vColour * texcol;
	DoAlphaTest(combinedcol);	
			
    gl_FragColor = combinedcol;
}

#version 120
#define LOWPREC 
#define lowp
#define mediump
#define highp
#define precision
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSL_ 1
//
// SDF (with effects) fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

// SDF values are measured from 0 (at the outer edge) to 1 which is the innermost point that can be represented
uniform bool gm_SDF_DrawGlow;				// whether the glow effect is enabled
uniform vec2 gm_SDF_Glow_MinMax;			// the SDF range across which the glow fades
uniform vec4 gm_SDF_Glow_Col;				// the colour of the glow

uniform bool gm_SDF_DrawOutline;			// whether the outline effect is enabled
uniform float gm_SDF_Outline_Thresh;		// the SDF distance which represents the outer edge of the outline
uniform vec4 gm_SDF_Outline_Col;			// the colour of the outline

uniform float gm_SDF_Core_Thresh;			// the SDF distance which represents the outer edge the shape
uniform vec4 gm_SDF_Core_Col;				// the colour of the core part of the shape

void main()
{
	vec4 texcol = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );
		
	float pixelspread = fwidth(texcol.a);	
	pixelspread = max(pixelspread * 0.75, 0.001);	
	
	float blendfactor;
	vec4 currcol = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, -1.0);
	
	// Handle glow effect
	if (gm_SDF_DrawGlow)
	{		
		if (texcol.a > gm_SDF_Glow_MinMax.x)
		{
			currcol = gm_SDF_Glow_Col;
			currcol.a *= smoothstep(gm_SDF_Glow_MinMax.x, gm_SDF_Glow_MinMax.y, texcol.a);
		}
	}	
	
	// Handle outline effect
	if (gm_SDF_DrawOutline)
	{
		if (texcol.a > (gm_SDF_Outline_Thresh - pixelspread))
		{			
			blendfactor = smoothstep(gm_SDF_Outline_Thresh - pixelspread, gm_SDF_Outline_Thresh + pixelspread, texcol.a);
			if (currcol.a < 0.0)
			{
				currcol = vec4(gm_SDF_Outline_Col.r,gm_SDF_Outline_Col.g,gm_SDF_Outline_Col.b, 0.0);
			}
			currcol = mix(currcol, gm_SDF_Outline_Col, blendfactor);
		}
	}
	
	// Handle inner core
	blendfactor = smoothstep(gm_SDF_Core_Thresh - pixelspread, gm_SDF_Core_Thresh + pixelspread, texcol.a);
	
	if (currcol.a < 0.0)
	{
		currcol = vec4(gm_SDF_Core_Col.r,gm_SDF_Core_Col.g,gm_SDF_Core_Col.b, 0.0);
	}
	texcol = mix(currcol, gm_SDF_Core_Col, blendfactor);	
	
	vec4 combinedcol = v_vColour * texcol;
	DoAlphaTest(combinedcol);	
			
    gl_FragColor = combinedcol;
}

__yy_sdf_blur_shader
precision mediump float;
#define LOWPREC lowp
// Uniforms look like they're shared between vertex and fragment shaders in GLSL, so we have to be careful to avoid name clashes

uniform sampler2D gm_BaseTexture;

uniform bool gm_PS_FogEnabled;
uniform vec4 gm_FogColour;
uniform bool gm_AlphaTestEnabled;
uniform float gm_AlphaRefValue;

void DoAlphaTest(vec4 SrcColour)
{
	if (gm_AlphaTestEnabled)
	{
		if (SrcColour.a <= gm_AlphaRefValue)
		{
			discard;
		}
	}
}

void DoFog(inout vec4 SrcColour, float fogval)
{
	if (gm_PS_FogEnabled)
	{
		SrcColour = mix(SrcColour, gm_FogColour, clamp(fogval, 0.0, 1.0)); 
	}
}

#define _YY_GLSLES_ 1
//
// SDF (with blur) fragment shader
//
varying vec2 v_vTexcoord;
varying vec4 v_vColour;

// SDF values are measured from 0 (at the outer edge) to 1 which is the innermost point that can be represented
uniform vec2 gm_SDF_Blur_MinMax;			// the range across which to filter the SDF
uniform vec4 gm_SDF_Blur_Col;				// the colour tint of the blurred text

void main()
{
	vec4 texcol = texture2D( gm_BaseTexture, v_vTexcoord );	
	vec4 currcol = gm_SDF_Blur_Col;
		
	currcol.a *= smoothstep(gm_SDF_Blur_MinMax.x, gm_SDF_Blur_MinMax.y, texcol.a);		
	
	vec4 combinedcol = v_vColour * currcol;
	DoAlphaTest(combinedcol);	

    gl_FragColor = combinedcol;
}
